{"session": "01a0f770-3a1d-7950-9e6c-44189fcc9fb2", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:46:13.485Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f780-43ed-7530-9e44-33c786c02c78", "call_id": "call_mzKlvzLGOb62ASHnoMgyAjQH", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"9b8e72\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000002666,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":6373,\"output\":\"# Source-comparison report\\n\\nReviewed the complete `draft-2.md` against the complete supplied `source.md`. The source is Swedish; the draft is American English. The source establishes Sanna Ek as the author and Öppna beslut as the commissioning association. No direct quotations occur in the draft. This report judges source support, including meaning carried across languages; it does not perform an editorial or mechanical review.\\n\\n## Complete claim accounting\\n\\nEach entry gives the draft passage, the source wording in its original language, and the comparison. Source quotations include relevant modifiers and qualifications. Advocacy and the author's reasoning are identified separately from factual assertions.\\n\\n### Title\\n\\n**Draft:** “Lervik should delay ending phone bookings”\\n\\n**Source:** “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik.” “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The title expresses the supplied position. “Lervik” stands for the municipal decision maker in a headline; it does not claim that residents collectively decide, that a binding decision has already been made, or that phone bookings have already ended. The body expressly identifies the board and the proposal. “Should delay” preserves advocacy rather than describing an accomplished event. No defect.\\n\\n### Summary\\n\\n**Draft:** “A report on an eight-week pilot in two association venues counts bookings rather than unique people.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler.” “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Duration, venue count, subject of measurement, and exclusion of unique-person measurement match. “Association venues” carries forward “föreningslokaler” from the report title and brief. No resident count, individual count, attendance count, or implied count of distinct phone users is added. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “Lervik’s municipal executive board should weigh staff workload against the value of each booking channel before making a permanent change for all seven venues.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the author's advocated evaluation, grounded in the supplied position and proposed measurements. The seven-venue scope and permanent-change qualification are supported. “Staff workload” presents the time-use concern, while the article later explicitly retains “labor cost” and “value to users.” “Value of each booking channel” is an argumentative formulation of the value to users of keeping a channel, not a claim that a particular channel has measured value. It does not turn time measurements into an established monetary calculation. No defect.\\n\\n### Byline\\n\\n**Draft:** “By Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut”\\n\\n**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Name, affiliation, and role match. “Spokesperson” does not add an office, elected position, or natural-gender claim. No defect.\\n\\n### Opening paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “The staff report prepared for the municipal executive board’s June 18 meeting proposes removing phone booking at all seven association venues from September.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” The location type is supplied in “ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler” and “Bokning av föreningslokaler.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The report, meeting date, proposed rather than completed action, affected channel, all-seven scope, and September start match. The draft adds no year to June or September and does not claim that the board approved the proposal. “Prepared for” is consistent with “inför” in this context. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “I urge the board to postpone a permanent switch to digital booking alone and first run a six-month trial with both phone and web booking.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** First-person advocacy belongs to the supplied author. Permanence, digital-only proposal, sequencing, six-month length, and retained phone/web channels match. “Urge” describes the argument being made in this article, not an invented earlier communication or personal event. No defect.\\n\\n### First heading\\n\\n**Draft:** “The report does not measure digital familiarity”\\n\\n**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The heading names one of the expressly unmeasured attributes. It preserves the report's measurement scope and does not imply that no phone user has digital experience. No defect.\\n\\n### Pilot results paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “The municipality’s pilot report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated April 8, 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 phone bookings during an eight-week trial in two venues.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Actual source, report title, report date, counts, channels, eight-week duration, and two-venue scope match. The count figures are expressly attributed to the report. No unsupported size evaluation, percentage, distinct-user inference, or future outcome is added. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “These are bookings, not unique people.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “These” refers to the preceding 96 and 24 report counts. The thing measured and the exclusion match. No defect.\\n\\n### Measurement and inference paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “The report does not measure age, functional ability, or familiarity with digital technology.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The three expressly unmeasured attributes match. “Functional ability” does not add a diagnosis, disability category, or prevalence claim. In this booking context “familiarity with digital technology” expresses the experience/familiarity dimension of “digital vana”; it does not state inability to use a particular system. The absence is limited to what the report measures, preserving the state of knowledge. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “Those 24 phone bookings cannot establish what proportion of residents cannot book online.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The number remains a booking count. “Cannot establish” preserves the supplied limit on inference, including population scope, rather than asserting that residents can or cannot use digital booking. “Online” refers to the web route already named in the same paragraph and throughout this two-channel proposal. The draft does not use the count to assign digital inability to 24 individuals. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “A phone booking is a reason to ask why that channel is used, not proof that someone cannot use the web.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ‘Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.’ Detta får citeras eller refereras.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The statement is the author's supplied position, paraphrased outside quotation marks. Its distinction between a reason to investigate and proof of inability matches. The draft reports neither a known motive nor a finding about actual users' capacities. The present use of the channel is supported by the trial bookings; omission of “fortfarande” does not create a new event or reverse the source's chronology. No defect.\\n\\n### Second heading\\n\\n**Draft:** “The workload argument needs a measurement”\\n\\n**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the author's supported argument that the proposed workload justification needs measurement. It does not assert a quantified burden or a proven saving. No defect.\\n\\n### Administration paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “The staff report gives a concrete reason for removing phone access: avoiding the work of entering information through two workflows.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The motive is attributed to the staff report and matches its administrative rationale. In context “phone access” means access to the phone booking route, as both the immediately preceding material and the subsequent trial proposal specify; it does not claim a proposal to remove all telephone contact with the municipality. “Concrete” identifies the stated, specific activity rather than evaluating its amount. No unsupported user motive, blame, expense claim, or proven workload reduction is introduced. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “That administrative concern deserves an answer.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the author's own evaluative acknowledgment of the supported concern, not an additional historical or technical claim. No defect.\\n\\n### Missing measurement and judgment paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “But the documents contain no time measurements or calculation of financial savings.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Both absent items and their documentary scope match. The draft does not say that no measurements or calculations have ever been made elsewhere, that no savings are possible, or that the administration knows the proposal will save nothing. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “A workload argument without time measurements in the decision documents is an inadequate basis for a permanent switch.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” “Författaren ska kunna kritisera beslutsunderlaget skarpt utan falska motiv eller tandlös neutralisering.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Inadequate basis” is the author's permitted sharp assessment of the supported absence of time measurements, not an invented legal requirement, institutional finding, or measured effect. The claim about absent measurements stays confined to the decision documents. Permanence is preserved. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “I do not oppose digital booking; the board must first be able to weigh the labor cost against the value to users.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna. Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” The author identification is “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Both attributed positions belong to Sanna Ek and match the supplied meaning and strength. “Must” expresses the author's position rather than a legal obligation. The board is the named municipal decision maker in the brief; “first” preserves sequencing. No defect.\\n\\n### Final heading\\n\\n**Draft:** “The board should authorize a trial and assess its cost”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” “Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is advocated action by the specified decision maker. It does not claim authorization already exists, a cost estimate has been supplied, or a particular budget is available. Assessing cost is a supported request for the board's consideration of the unresolved cost. No defect.\\n\\n### Trial proposal paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “Öppna beslut proposes keeping both booking routes for six months at all seven venues.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Proposer, proposed status, duration, retained two routes, and all-seven scope match. Neither future enactment nor funding is asserted. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “Staff should record the time spent on each route and ask users to say voluntarily why they choose phone or web.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** These are the same proposed tasks during the trial described in the preceding sentence. “Staff” names the people who would perform the administration's recording and requests, without inventing a named official or department. “Should” remains part of the association's proposal. The per-route scope, voluntary qualification, and phone/web alternatives are retained. No compulsion, known motive, completed survey, or measured result is added. No defect.\\n\\n### Closing paragraph\\n\\n**Draft:** “Our association makes no claim to have funded the trial or estimated its cost.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The association's lack of a claim is preserved exactly as a lack of a claim. The draft does not convert it into certainty that no funding or estimate exists, that the association could not fund the trial, or that the trial will cost a particular amount. “Estimated its cost” conveys “kostnadsberäknat” here. “Our” places the supplied spokesperson in the association's speaking voice, without stating a separate membership office or personal financial contribution. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “The municipal executive board should postpone the permanent switch, assess the trial’s cost, and authorize it.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” “Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the supported recommended decision. It preserves permanent-switch postponement and cost consideration, then explicitly advocates authorization of the proposed trial. It does not attribute an authorization to the board or promise that the trial is funded or inexpensive. No defect.\\n\\n**Draft:** “Afterward, it can decide whether to remove, change, or retain a booking channel.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” The broader sequence is “kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The three possible channel decisions match. “It” refers to the board, and “can” preserves possibility rather than certainty of a particular later outcome. “Afterward” follows the preceding advocated cost assessment and trial authorization; the surrounding brief also establishes trying both channels before a permanent decision. It invents no date or result. No defect.\\n\\n## Pronoun, person, number, and natural-gender accounting\\n\\n- “I” in “I urge” and “I do not oppose” conveys first-person singular. The author is Sanna Ek, expressly identified in the source. The source supplies the position as Sanna's, using “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt” and “Hon.” English “I” adds no natural gender. The Swedish feminine “hon” is source information, but no unsupported natural-gender assertion is introduced or needed in the draft.\\n- “Our” in “Our association” conveys a first-person plural relationship to the association. The source establishes Sanna as its spokesperson and supplies the association's position. This institutional speaking voice does not enumerate members, require a named coauthor, assert a particular membership status, or add gender information.\\n- “Someone” is an indefinite singular person in the general evidentiary proposition. It corresponds to “någon” in “inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.” It identifies no actual individual and conveys no natural gender.\\n- “They” in “why they choose” refers to plural “users,” corresponding to “användarna” and “de väljer.” The plural number is supplied. No claim about an identified user's gender is made.\\n- “It” in “authorize it” refers to the singular trial; “its” in “assess its cost” likewise refers to that trial. These are singular, inanimate referents supported by “ett sex månader långt försök” and “Kostnaden.” No person or natural gender is asserted.\\n- “It” in “Afterward, it can decide” refers to the singular municipal executive board, matching “kommunstyrelsen” and “den.” Swedish common grammatical gender in “den” is not natural gender; English institutional “it” adds no human-gender claim.\\n- “These” and “Those” refer to plural bookings, with “Those 24” restricted to the phone bookings. Both counts and their booking units are supplied; the pronouns do not convert bookings into people.\\n- “That channel” refers to phone booking. “That administrative concern” refers to entering information through two workflows. Neither demonstrative introduces an additional entity or modifier.\\n- “Both,” “each,” and “all seven” convey their stated scopes: the two supplied phone/web channels, measurement per route, and all seven supplied venues. “The board,” “the municipality,” “the report,” and “the staff report” maintain their identified singular institutional or documentary referents.\\n\\n## Meaning and countercase tests\\n\\nThe wording changes below were tested for contextual differences, in both directions. A countercase must make the ordinary contextual statement true on one side and false on the other; different connotations or alternate meanings outside this context do not suffice.\\n\\n- **“Föreningslokaler” / “association venues”:** The draft names the same bookable premises used for associations, rather than specifying community centers, ownership, a legal property type, or a dedicated event-hall classification. A municipal meeting room bookable by an association falls under both contextual expressions; an association-owned bookable room also falls under both. Neither statement requires the latter ownership. No standing countercase changes the physical premises in this booking context.\\n- **“Digital vana” / “digital familiarity,” “familiarity with digital technology”:** Both name the relevant familiarity/experience attribute. A person unfamiliar with using digital systems is within both contextual readings; a practiced digital user is within both. The draft does not equate this attribute with technical knowledge, disability, age, or demonstrated booking competence. No concrete contextual distinction supporting a defect was found.\\n- **“Boka digitalt” / “book online”:** The supplied debate concerns the explicitly named phone and web booking routes: “ett halvår med både telefon och webb,” “96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon,” and “varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” Both sentences limit what can be inferred from the phone-booking count about using the web route. An offline digital system would be a different hypothetical route, not what either sentence names in this supplied two-route debate. Neither sentence says that the report establishes capacity for any other route. No defect.\\n- **“Tjänsteutlåtandet” / “staff report”:** Both refer to the same document prepared for the June 18 meeting and containing the stated proposal and motive. The draft does not claim a judicial ruling, board resolution, pilot result, or opinion attributed to another source. No standing countercase changes whose document or assertion is described.\\n- **“Två flöden” / “two workflows”:** The source itself supplies the administrative meaning: staff entering information and “dubbel administration.” Both expressions describe that work organization; neither requires a specific software architecture or quantifies duplication. No standing countercase was found.\\n- **“Kostnadsberäknat” / “estimated its cost”:** Both describe producing a calculation/estimate of trial cost. The relevant assertion remains that the association makes no claim to have done it. An estimate made elsewhere, or an estimate existing without an association claim, is compatible with both, demonstrating that the draft preserves rather than strengthens the source's epistemic limit.\\n- **“Arbetskostnaden” / “labor cost”:** These match in the body. The summary's “staff workload” is the author's advocated consideration of the supplied time-use issue, not an attributed assertion that workload and monetary labor cost are identical. Different pay rates could make equal time have unequal labor cost; that case does not falsify the summary's recommendation to weigh workload or the body's recommendation to weigh labor cost. It is therefore not a changed factual assertion requiring repair.\\n- **“Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till” / “should assess the trial's cost”:** The source demands a position on unresolved cost; the draft advocates assessing that cost as part of the decision. This is the author's supported recommendation, with no claimed completed assessment or specified amount. No factual countercase creates a defect.\\n\\n## Findings\\n\\n**Source-support findings:** None. All factual and attributed claims are supported within the supplied scope. The author's additional formulations are argument, evaluation, or advocacy resting on those supported facts and the supplied standpoint. The draft preserves the absence of individual counts, unmeasured user attributes, proposal status, the documentary limit on missing measurements, and uncertainty about funding and cost estimation.\\n\\n**Translation findings:** None. There are no translated direct quotations. The cross-language claims outside quotations convey the supplied referents and qualifications in their context without a supported meaning defect.\\n\\n**Completion status:** Complete. The entire draft, including title, summary, byline, headings, implications, and pronouns, has been compared with the entire supplied source.\\n\\n**Unresolved findings:** None.\\n\"}"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f770-3b92-7493-b6a2-035e600daa81", "create_time": 1790858773.48541}}}

{"session": "01a0f774-f840-7e22-9760-940233874a3c", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:34:01.248Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f775-17a0-7681-9ed5-96d1fd61fd7c", "call_id": "call_5cZwKWIWhUxL9s9B2hqonT0A", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "# Lervik should delay ending phone bookings\n\nAn eight-week pilot in two venues for local associations counted bookings rather than individual users. Lervik’s municipal executive board should weigh staff workload against the value of each booking channel before making a permanent change for all seven venues.\n\nBy Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut\n\nThe staff report prepared for the municipal executive board’s June 18 meeting proposes removing phone booking at all seven venues for local associations from September. I urge the board to postpone a permanent switch to online booking alone and first run a six-month trial with both phone and web booking.\n\n## The pilot does not measure digital familiarity\n\nThe municipality’s pilot report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated April 8, 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 phone bookings during an eight-week trial in two venues. These are bookings, not unique people.\n\nThe report does not measure age, functional ability, or familiarity with digital technology. Those 24 phone bookings cannot establish what proportion of residents cannot book online. A phone booking is a reason to ask why that channel is used, not proof that someone cannot use the web.\n\n## The workload argument needs a measurement\n\nThe staff report gives a concrete reason for removing phone access: avoiding the work of entering information through two workflows. That administrative concern deserves an answer.\n\nBut the documents contain no time measurements or calculation of financial savings. An unmeasured workload is an inadequate basis for a permanent switch. I do not oppose digital booking; the board must first be able to weigh the labor cost against the value to users.\n\n## The board should authorize a trial and assess its cost\n\nÖppna beslut proposes keeping both booking routes for six months at all seven venues. Staff should record the time spent on each route and ask users to say voluntarily why they choose phone or web.\n\nOur association makes no claim to have funded the trial or estimated its cost. The municipal executive board should postpone the permanent switch, assess the trial’s cost, and authorize it. Afterward, it can decide whether to remove, change, or retain a booking channel.\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "# Opinion brief and source\n\nSYNTHETIC EVALUATION MATERIAL. Fictional policy debate, not legal advice or a real municipal process. Source language: Swedish.\n\n## Brief\n\nSkriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb. En tydlig, sakligt skarp text på ungefär 400 ord. Markdown med orienterande rubriker. Ingen kommersiell CTA.\n\n## Underlag\n\nKommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.\n\nTjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.\n\nÖppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb. Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.\n\nSanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras. Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna. Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.\n\n## Boundaries / judging\n\nInga lagkrav, protester, diskrimineringsfall, partipolitiska motiv eller besparingar är belagda. Siffror ska tillskrivas rapporten där de bär argumentet. Författaren ska kunna kritisera beslutsunderlaget skarpt utan falska motiv eller tandlös neutralisering. Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.\n"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f774-f86f-7f50-84b5-6223f87cf05e", "create_time": 1790858041.248752}}}

The first two refer to plural booking/count referents; the latter two to singular nonhuman referents. |\\n+\\n+## Source-support findings and smallest supported repairs\\n+\\n+### F1 — Added locality of the associations\\n+\\n+Draft: “two venues for local associations” and “all seven venues for local associations.”\\n+\\n+Source: “föreningslokaler” and “alla sju lokaler.” The source identifies association premises, but does not locate or restrict the associations themselves.\\n+\\n+Difference: geographical scope of the beneficiaries. A municipality could offer association premises to nationwide associations with no local membership or branch. Conversely, premises intended for local associations are association premises, so that direction does not produce a counterexample. No supplied statement excludes the standing case.\\n+\\n+Smallest repair: replace “venues for local associations” with “association venues” in both occurrences.\\n+\\n+### F2 — The summary changes the counting subject\\n+\\n+Draft: “An eight-week pilot … counted bookings rather than individual users.”\\n+\\n+Source: “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.”\\n+\\n+Difference: subject and scope. The pilot could have collected a distinct-user count in an internal dataset while the published report counted only bookings. The source holds and the draft's characterization of the pilot as counting bookings rather than users fails. These are tests of the semantic scopes, not allegations about what occurred. {"session": "01a0f77b-6b29-77b2-9fc4-c39ee4b7fa97", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:41:03.900Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f77b-8a9c-74b0-b06b-3705d74b948a", "call_id": "call_borqYO8L6B8oLXdllhJyxcjj", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "# Lervik should delay ending phone bookings\n\nA report on an eight-week pilot in two association venues counts bookings rather than unique people. Lervik’s municipal executive board should weigh staff workload against the value of each booking channel before making a permanent change for all seven venues.\n\nBy Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut\n\nThe staff report prepared for the municipal executive board’s June 18 meeting proposes removing phone booking at all seven association venues from September. I urge the board to postpone a permanent switch to digital booking alone and first run a six-month trial with both phone and web booking.\n\n## The report does not measure digital familiarity\n\nThe municipality’s pilot report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated April 8, 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 phone bookings during an eight-week trial in two venues. These are bookings, not unique people.\n\nThe report does not measure age, functional ability, or familiarity with digital technology. Those 24 phone bookings cannot establish what proportion of residents cannot book online. A phone booking is a reason to ask why that channel is used, not proof that someone cannot use the web.\n\n## The workload argument needs a measurement\n\nThe staff report gives a concrete reason for removing phone access: avoiding the work of entering information through two workflows. That administrative concern deserves an answer.\n\nBut the documents contain no time measurements or calculation of financial savings. A workload argument without time measurements in the decision documents is an inadequate basis for a permanent switch. I do not oppose digital booking; the board must first be able to weigh the labor cost against the value to users.\n\n## The board should authorize a trial and assess its cost\n\nÖppna beslut proposes keeping both booking routes for six months at all seven venues. Staff should record the time spent on each route and ask users to say voluntarily why they choose phone or web.\n\nOur association makes no claim to have funded the trial or estimated its cost. The municipal executive board should postpone the permanent switch, assess the trial’s cost, and authorize it. Afterward, it can decide whether to remove, change, or retain a booking channel.\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "# Opinion brief and source\n\nSYNTHETIC EVALUATION MATERIAL. Fictional policy debate, not legal advice or a real municipal process. Source language: Swedish.\n\n## Brief\n\nSkriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb. En tydlig, sakligt skarp text på ungefär 400 ord. Markdown med orienterande rubriker. Ingen kommersiell CTA.\n\n## Underlag\n\nKommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.\n\nTjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.\n\nÖppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb. Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.\n\nSanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras. Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna. Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.\n\n## Boundaries / judging\n\nInga lagkrav, protester, diskrimineringsfall, partipolitiska motiv eller besparingar är belagda. Siffror ska tillskrivas rapporten där de bär argumentet. Författaren ska kunna kritisera beslutsunderlaget skarpt utan falska motiv eller tandlös neutralisering. Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.\n"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f77b-6b5c-7132-81e2-26935159346a", "create_time": 1790858463.900385}}}

It does not claim authorization already exists, a cost estimate has been supplied, or a particular budget is available. Assessing cost is a supported request for the board's consideration of the unresolved cost. No defect.\\n+\\n+### Trial proposal paragraph\\n+\\n+**Draft:** “Öppna beslut proposes keeping both booking routes for six months at all seven venues.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Proposer, proposed status, duration, retained two routes, and all-seven scope match. Neither future enactment nor funding is asserted. No defect.\\n+\\n+**Draft:** “Staff should record the time spent on each route and ask users to say voluntarily why they choose phone or web.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** These are the same proposed tasks during the trial described in the preceding sentence. “Staff” names the people who would perform the administration's recording and requests, without inventing a named official or department. “Should” remains part of the association's proposal. The per-route scope, voluntary qualification, and phone/web alternatives are retained. No compulsion, known motive, completed survey, or measured result is added. No defect.\\n+\\n+### Closing paragraph\\n+\\n+**Draft:** “Our association makes no claim to have funded the trial or estimated its cost.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The association's lack of a claim is preserved exactly as a lack of a claim. The draft does not convert it into certainty that no funding or estimate exists, that the association could not fund the trial, or that the trial will cost a particular amount. “Estimated its cost” conveys “kostnadsberäknat” here. “Our” places the supplied spokesperson in the association's speaking voice, without stating a separate membership office or personal financial contribution. No defect.\\n+\\n+**Draft:** “The municipal executive board should postpone the permanent switch, assess the trial’s cost, and authorize it.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” “Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** This is the supported recommended decision. It preserves permanent-switch postponement and cost consideration, then explicitly advocates authorization of the proposed trial. It does not attribute an authorization to the board or promise that the trial is funded or inexpensive. No defect.\\n+\\n+**Draft:** “Afterward, it can decide whether to remove, change, or retain a booking channel.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” The broader sequence is “kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The three possible channel decisions match. “It” refers to the board, and “can” preserves possibility rather than certainty of a particular later outcome. “Afterward” follows the preceding advocated cost assessment and trial authorization; the surrounding brief also establishes trying both channels before a permanent decision. It invents no date or result. No defect.\\n+\\n+## Pronoun, person, number, and natural-gender accounting\\n+\\n+- “I” in “I urge” and “I do not oppose” conveys first-person singular. The author is Sanna Ek, expressly identified in the source. The source supplies the position as Sanna's, using “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt” and “Hon.” English “I” adds no natural gender. The Swedish feminine “hon” is source information, but no unsupported natural-gender assertion is introduced or needed in the draft.\\n+- “Our” in “Our association” conveys a first-person plural relationship to the association. The source establishes Sanna as its spokesperson and supplies the association's position. This institutional speaking voice does not enumerate members, require a named coauthor, assert a particular membership status, or add gender information.\\n+- “Someone” is an indefinite singular person in the general evidentiary proposition. It corresponds to “någon” in “inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.” It identifies no actual individual and conveys no natural gender.\\n+- “They” in “why they choose” refers to plural “users,” corresponding to “användarna” and “de väljer.” The plural number is supplied. No claim about an identified user's gender is made.\\n+- “It” in “authorize it” refers to the singular trial; “its” in “assess its cost” likewise refers to that trial. These are singular, inanimate referents supported by “ett sex månader långt försök” and “Kostnaden.” No person or natural gender is asserted.\\n+- “It” in “Afterward, it can decide” refers to the singular municipal executive board, matching “kommunstyrelsen” and “den.” Swedish common grammatical gender in “den” is not natural gender; English institutional “it” adds no human-gender claim.\\n+- “These” and “Those” refer to plural bookings, with “Those 24” restricted to the phone bookings. Both counts and their booking units are supplied; the pronouns do not convert bookings into people.\\n+- “That channel” refers to phone booking. “That administrative concern” refers to entering information through two workflows. Neither demonstrative introduces an additional entity or modifier.\\n+- “Both,” “each,” and “all seven” convey their stated scopes: the two supplied phone/web channels, measurement per route, and all seven supplied venues. “The board,” “the municipality,” “the report,” and “the staff report” maintain their identified singular institutional or documentary referents.\\n+\\n+## Meaning and countercase tests\\n+\\n+The wording changes below were tested for contextual differences, in both directions. A countercase must make the ordinary contextual statement true on one side and false on the other; different connotations or alternate meanings outside this context do not suffice.\\n+\\n+- **“Föreningslokaler” / “association venues”:** The draft names the same bookable premises used for associations, rather than specifying community centers, ownership, a legal property type, or a dedicated event-hall classification. A municipal meeting room bookable by an association falls under both contextual expressions; an association-owned bookable room also falls under both. Neither statement requires the latter ownership. No standing countercase changes the physical premises in this booking context.\\n+- **“Digital vana” / “digital familiarity,” “familiarity with digital technology”:** Both name the relevant familiarity/experience attribute. A person unfamiliar with using digital systems is within both contextual readings; a practiced digital user is within both. The draft does not equate this attribute with technical knowledge, disability, age, or demonstrated booking competence. No concrete contextual distinction supporting a defect was found.\\n+- **“Boka digitalt” / “book online”:** The supplied debate concerns the explicitly named phone and web booking routes: “ett halvår med både telefon och webb,” “96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon,” and “varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” Both sentences limit what can be inferred from the phone-booking count about using the web route. An offline digital system would be a different hypothetical route, not what either sentence names in this supplied two-route debate. Neither sentence says that the report establishes capacity for any other route. No defect.\\n+- **“Tjänsteutlåtandet” / “staff report”:** Both refer to the same document prepared for the June 18 meeting and containing the stated proposal and motive. The draft does not claim a judicial ruling, board resolution, pilot result, or opinion attributed to another source. No standing countercase changes whose document or assertion is described.\\n+- **“Två flöden” / “two workflows”:** The source itself supplies the administrative meaning: staff entering information and “dubbel administration.” Both expressions describe that work organization; neither requires a specific software architecture or quantifies duplication. No standing countercase was found.\\n+- **“Kostnadsberäknat” / “estimated its cost”:** Both describe producing a calculation/estimate of trial cost. The relevant assertion remains that the association makes no claim to have done it. An estimate made elsewhere, or an estimate existing without an association claim, is compatible with both, demonstrating that the draft preserves rather than strengthens the source's epistemic limit.\\n+- **“Arbetskostnaden” / “labor cost”:** These match in the body. The summary's “staff workload” is the author's advocated consideration of the supplied time-use issue, not an attributed assertion that workload and monetary labor cost are identical. Different pay rates could make equal time have unequal labor cost; that case does not falsify the summary's recommendation to weigh workload or the body's recommendation to weigh labor cost. It is therefore not a changed factual assertion requiring repair.\\n+- **“Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till” / “should assess the trial's cost”:** The source demands a position on unresolved cost; the draft advocates assessing that cost as part of the decision. This is the author's supported recommendation, with no claimed completed assessment or specified amount. No factual countercase creates a defect.\\n+\\n+## Findings\\n+\\n+**Source-support findings:** None. All factual and attributed claims are supported within the supplied scope. The author's additional formulations are argument, evaluation, or advocacy resting on those supported facts and the supplied standpoint. The draft preserves the absence of individual counts, unmeasured user attributes, proposal status, the documentary limit on missing measurements, and uncertainty about funding and cost estimation.\\n+\\n+**Translation findings:** None. There are no translated direct quotations. The cross-language claims outside quotations convey the supplied referents and qualifications in their context without a supported meaning defect.\\n+\\n+**Completion status:** Complete. The entire draft, including title, summary, byline, headings, implications, and pronouns, has been compared with the entire supplied source.\\n+\\n+**Unresolved findings:** None.\\n*** End Patch\"));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f77b-6b5c-7132-81e2-26935159346a", "create_time": 1790858536.315565}}}
